# TOL — Tolerance analysis

**CATIA-class equivalent:** 3D tolerance stack-up (e.g. 3DCS-class add-ons)  
**Autevra workspace:** Production → stack-ups  
**Scope:** Shared  
**Cases:** 712 (Have 668, Partial 12, Gap 32)

1D/3D stack-ups by worst case, RSS and Monte Carlo.

[← Back to the index](../README.md)

## Features

- 1D stack-up — 668 cases (Have)
- Monte Carlo — 12 cases (Partial)
- 3D stack and GD&T — 32 cases (Gap)

## Monte Carlo

Status in Autevra: **Partial**

| ID | Test case | Type | Pri | Steps | Expected result | Data / reference |
